&lt;li&gt;Renamed file drop emitted events to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri://drag-enter&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri://drag-over&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri://drag-drop&lt;/code&gt;, and &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri://drag-leave&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Renamed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::WebviewWindow::disable_file_drop_handler&lt;/code&gt; to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::WebviewWindow::disable_drag_drop_handler&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Changed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::WebviewWindow::url&lt;/code&gt; getter to return a result.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Changed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::Env.args_os&lt;/code&gt;, to include the binary path, previously it was skipped.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Renamed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;getAll&lt;/code&gt; and &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;getCurrent&lt;/code&gt; to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;getAllWindows&lt;/code&gt; and &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;getCurrentWindow&lt;/code&gt; in the JS &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;window&lt;/code&gt; module but you probably want &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;getAllWebviewWindows&lt;/code&gt; and &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;getCurrentWebviewWindow&lt;/code&gt; from the &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;webviewWindow&lt;/code&gt; module.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;removed&quot;&gt;Removed&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;The &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;reqwest-*&lt;/code&gt; Cargo features were removed&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;UpdaterEvent&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed&lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::api&lt;/code&gt; module and moved them into standalone plugins in &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;plugins-workspace&lt;/code&gt; repo.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::scope::IpcScope&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::scope::ipc&lt;/code&gt; module and all its types.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::scope::FsScope&lt;/code&gt;, use &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::scope::fs::Scope&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::scope::GlobPattern&lt;/code&gt;, use &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::scope::fs::Pattern&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::scope::FsScopeEvent&lt;/code&gt;, use &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::scope::fs::Event&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::scope::HttpScope&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::scope::ShellScope&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::scope::ShellScopeAllowedCommand&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::scope::ShellScopeAllowedArg&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::scope::ExecuteArgs&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::scope::ShellScopeConfig&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::scope::ShellScopeError&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;linux-protocol-headers&lt;/code&gt; cargo feature flag, now enabled by default.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::path::Error&lt;/code&gt; and &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::path::Result&lt;/code&gt; and added its variants to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::Error&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::path::Result&lt;/code&gt; and &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::plugin::Result&lt;/code&gt; aliases, you should use &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::Result&lt;/code&gt; or your own &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;Result&lt;/code&gt; type.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Changed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::Builder::on_page_load&lt;/code&gt; handler to take references. The page load hook is now triggered for load started and finished events, to determine what triggered it see &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::PageLoadPayload::event&lt;/code&gt; field.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::GlobalWindowEvent&lt;/code&gt; struct, and unpacked its fields to be passed directly to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::Builder::on_window_event&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::EventHandler&lt;/code&gt; type.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Renamed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::Context::default_window_icon_mut&lt;/code&gt; to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri::Context::set_default_window_icon&lt;/code&gt; and changed it to accept &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;Option&amp;#x3C;T&gt;&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;config-restructure&quot;&gt;Config restructure&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p&gt;Restructured Tauri config per &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/rfcs/blob/f3e82a6b0c5390401e855850d47dc7b7d9afd684/texts/0005-tauri-config-restructure.md&quot;&gt;RFC#5&lt;/a&gt;:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Moved &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;package.productName&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;package.version&lt;/code&gt; and &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri.bundle.identifier&lt;/code&gt; fields to the top-level.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;package&lt;/code&gt; object.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Renamed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri&lt;/code&gt; object to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;app&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Moved &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri.bundle&lt;/code&gt; object to the top-level.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Renamed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;build.distDir&lt;/code&gt; field to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;frontendDist&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Renamed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;build.devPath&lt;/code&gt; field to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;devUrl&lt;/code&gt; and will no longer accepts paths, it will only accept URLs.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Moved &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri.pattern&lt;/code&gt; to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;app.security.pattern&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri.bundle.updater&lt;/code&gt; object, and its fields have been moved to the updater plugin under &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;plugins.updater&lt;/code&gt; object.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Moved &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;build.withGlobalTauri&lt;/code&gt; to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;app.withGlobalTauri&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Moved &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri.bundle.dmg&lt;/code&gt; object to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;bundle.macOS.dmg&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Moved &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri.bundle.deb&lt;/code&gt; object to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;bundle.linux.deb&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Moved &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri.bundle.appimage&lt;/code&gt; object to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;bundle.linux.appimage&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Removed all license fields from each bundle configuration object and instead added &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;bundle.license&lt;/code&gt; and &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;bundle.licenseFile&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Renamed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;AppUrl&lt;/code&gt; to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;FrontendDist&lt;/code&gt; and refactored its variants to be more explicit.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Renamed &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri.window.fileDropEnabeld&lt;/code&gt; to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;app.window.dragDropEnabled&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;/details&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;migration&quot;&gt;Migration&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;As we try to make the migration from previous Tauri versions as smooth as possible, we have documentation available to guide you through the process.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;If you are migrating from a 1.x release please check out this &lt;a href=&quot;https://v2.tauri.app/start/migrate/from-tauri-1/&quot;&gt;migration guide&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;For upgrading from a 2.0 beta or release candidate version check out this &lt;a href=&quot;https://v2.tauri.app/start/migrate/from-tauri-2-beta/&quot;&gt;migration guide&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The Tauri v2 CLI includes a &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;migrate&lt;/code&gt; command that automates most of the process and helps you finish the migration:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;starlight-tabs data-sync-key=&quot;cmd&quot;&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;ul role=&quot;tablist&quot;&gt;&lt;li role=&quot;presentation&quot;&gt;&lt;a role=&quot;tab&quot; href=&quot;#tab-panel-4185&quot; id=&quot;tab-4185&quot; aria-selected=&quot;true&quot; tabindex=&quot;0&quot;&gt;npm&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li role=&quot;presentation&quot;&gt;&lt;a role=&quot;tab&quot; href=&quot;#tab-panel-4186&quot; id=&quot;tab-4186&quot; aria-selected=&quot;false&quot; tabindex=&quot;-1&quot;&gt;yarn&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li role=&quot;presentation&quot;&gt;&lt;a role=&quot;tab&quot; href=&quot;#tab-panel-4187&quot; id=&quot;tab-4187&quot; aria-selected=&quot;false&quot; tabindex=&quot;-1&quot;&gt;pnpm&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li role=&quot;presentation&quot;&gt;&lt;a role=&quot;tab&quot; href=&quot;#tab-panel-4188&quot; id=&quot;tab-4188&quot; aria-selected=&quot;false&quot; tabindex=&quot;-1&quot;&gt;cargo&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;tab-panel-4185&quot; aria-labelledby=&quot;tab-4185&quot; role=&quot;tabpanel&quot;&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;npm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;install&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/cli@next&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;npm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;run&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;migrate&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;tab-panel-4186&quot; aria-labelledby=&quot;tab-4186&quot; role=&quot;tabpanel&quot; hidden&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;yarn&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;upgrade&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/cli@next&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;yarn&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;migrate&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;tab-panel-4187&quot; aria-labelledby=&quot;tab-4187&quot; role=&quot;tabpanel&quot; hidden&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;pnpm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;update&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/cli@next&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;pnpm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;migrate&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;tab-panel-4188&quot; aria-labelledby=&quot;tab-4188&quot; role=&quot;tabpanel&quot; hidden&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;cargo&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;install&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri-cli&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--version&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;^2.0.0&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--locked&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;cargo&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;migrate&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;starlight-tabs-restore&gt;&lt;/starlight-tabs-restore&gt;&lt;/starlight-tabs&gt;&lt;aside aria-label=&quot;Rust Migration&quot;&gt;&lt;p aria-hidden=&quot;true&quot;&gt;Rust Migration&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;p&gt;We can not automatically migrate your Rust code, so make sure to go through the documentation and rust docs of the 2.0 version.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/aside&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;call-to-action&quot;&gt;Call To Action&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;If you are familiar with Tauri and have used it already during your journey, please take your time to check out the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/discussions&quot;&gt;Github Discussions&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/issues&quot;&gt;Github Issues&lt;/a&gt;. Maybe you have already solved the issues your fellow newcomers to Tauri are experiencing right now.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;If you think that some of these problems you have seen are generic and should be documented somewhere we probably have the perfect place for it in our official &lt;a href=&quot;https://v2.tauri.app&quot;&gt;documentation&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;To contribute improvements or additions we are open for PRs in the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri-docs&quot;&gt;tauri-docs&lt;/a&gt; repository. Please make sure you’ve read the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri-docs/blob/v2/.github/CONTRIBUTING.md&quot;&gt;guidelines&lt;/a&gt; for contribution though.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;If you are in the position to understand and translate the current documentation into your native language we appreciate &lt;a href=&quot;https://v2.tauri.app/contribute/translate-status&quot;&gt;content translations&lt;/a&gt; to our documentation.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The repositories surrounding Tauri are also looking for contributors, especially we would love more maintainers and contributors to the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/plugins-workspace&quot;&gt;&lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;plugin-workspace&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The plugins are now a major part of the development and user experience of Tauri and all kind of help is welcome there. From discussing new plugin ideas, collaborating with others to write new plugins, contributing PRs to fix bugs in existing plugins or documenting weird workarounds and knowledge in the plugin readme or code.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;roadmap&quot;&gt;Roadmap&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;You probably expect solid plans for the future and new cool ideas from us.
We currently have some in mind but have not committed to a roadmap beyond 2.x yet.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We mainly want to focus on improving this major version with a better developer experience, better documentation and less impactful bugs. We want to improve especially the mobile development experience and make the whole flow from idea to published application as seamless as possible.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Things on our radar for the future we feel we should mention at least:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Providing or Bundling Chromium Embedded Framework (CEF) for Linux as an alternative to WebKit2GTK&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/servo/servo&quot;&gt;Servo&lt;/a&gt; as Tauri WebView (&lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/wry/tree/servo-wry-demo&quot;&gt;POC in Wry&lt;/a&gt;)&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;p&gt;If you want to collaborate on these ideas, please let us know and we will figure it out together.&lt;/p&gt;</content:encoded></item><item><title>Tauri 2.0 Release Candidate</title><link>https://v2.tauri.app/blog/tauri-2-0-0-release-candidate/</link><guid isPermaLink="true">https://v2.tauri.app/blog/tauri-2-0-0-release-candidate/</guid><description>Tauri 2.0 is on track to stable and now enters the release candidate phase. Read more about breaking changes and our plans for stable.</description><pubDate>Thu, 01 Aug 2024 00:00:00 GMT</pubDate><content:encoded>&lt;p&gt;We are very proud to finally announce the first release candidate for the new major version of Tauri.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;After over half a year of beta versions, following over a year of alpha versions we are finally at the point where we consider Tauri 2 stabilized and do no longer expect breaking changes.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We want to use a comparably short release candidate time frame to focus on our documentation and important bug fixes, which have been reported by our awesome community and working group members.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;A simplified &lt;a href=&quot;#too-long-didnt-read&quot;&gt;TL;DR&lt;/a&gt; can be found at the end of this post.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;the-road-to-stable-and-beyond&quot;&gt;The Road to Stable and Beyond&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;With this release candidate we want to communicate our expectations and timeline for the stable release.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We have been asked countless times “&lt;em&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://englishinprogress.net/blog/wen-instead-of-when/&quot;&gt;Wen&lt;/a&gt; Tauri 2.0?&lt;/em&gt;” and always gave broad answers. Especially in open source projects overpromising can be a quick way to burn out developers and maintainers or lead to angry comments from disappointed adopters.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;This is one of the reasons for the long alpha and beta stage and why we waited with the release candidate, as we strive to get things right and simple to use.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Another reason is that we made the mistake of overpromising this major version with “&lt;em&gt;mobile as a first class citizen&lt;/em&gt;” and realized over the past months that we
can only build the foundation for mobile on our own and need to iterate on this together with the community and our adopters to get it right.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;This doesn’t mean that mobile is broken and unsupported. We have mobile plugins in our official plugin repository and have seen developers who have built cool apps on Android and iOS with Tauri.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Our partner &lt;a href=&quot;https://crabnebula.dev&quot;&gt;CrabNebula&lt;/a&gt; also provided us with feedback on how easy (or complicated) the developer experience was when they built or supported mobile applications for customers. They even contributed multiple mobile plugins (&lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/plugins-workspace/tree/v2/plugins/nfc&quot;&gt;NFC&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/plugins-workspace/tree/v2/plugins/barcode-scanner&quot;&gt;Barcode Scanner&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/plugins-workspace/tree/v2/plugins/biometric&quot;&gt;Biometric&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/plugins-workspace/tree/v2/plugins/haptics&quot;&gt;Haptics&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/plugins-workspace/tree/v2/plugins/geolocation&quot;&gt;Geolocation&lt;/a&gt;) as part of their work.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We see improvements to be made in the development experience for mobile and we acknowledge that not all of our desktop features and plugins are ported or available on mobile yet.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;This causes us to say that we don’t want to raise expectations that Tauri 2.0 will be &lt;em&gt;the&lt;/em&gt; “&lt;em&gt;mobile as a first class citizen&lt;/em&gt;” release but we want to make clear that you can develop production ready mobile applications with Tauri &lt;strong&gt;NOW&lt;/strong&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;What you can expect from stable after this release candidate is:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Clearer and comprehensive documentation&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Less critical bugs preventing productive usage&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We plan to release the stable version for 2.0 in the end of August. This will, at the time of writing, allow for a ~4 week release candidate cycle.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;After the stable release our focus will be shifting to providing feature parity wherever possible and to improve the development process for mobile. This will happen in minor releases of Tauri.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Feature parity and plugin development will be aligned with major versions of Tauri but will be mostly independent from Tauri core features and happen in our &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/plugins-workspace&quot;&gt;plugin-workspace&lt;/a&gt; repository.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Developer experience is a very important topic for us. If you have improvement suggestions or want to improve the status quo on your own please do not hesitate to reach out with PRs, issues or friendly conversations on our discord server.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;breaking-changes&quot;&gt;Breaking Changes&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Before we enter the “&lt;em&gt;no more breaking changes&lt;/em&gt;” expectation phase we discussed and planned some from our perspective necessary breaking changes a while ago.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;These changes affect a lot of developers, so we wanted to bundle them and make it as painless as possible to upgrade from the latest beta to release candidate or stable.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;For app developers we have breaking changes in how core plugins are referenced in the permissions.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;You should be able to automatically migrate from the latest beta to release candidate. For this to succeed you must be sure to be on the latest (RC not beta) version of the Tauri &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/releases?q=tauri-cli&amp;#x26;expanded=false&quot;&gt;CLI&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;aside aria-label=&quot;Automated Migrate&quot;&gt;&lt;p aria-hidden=&quot;true&quot;&gt;Automated Migrate&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;starlight-tabs data-sync-key=&quot;cmd&quot;&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;ul role=&quot;tablist&quot;&gt;&lt;li role=&quot;presentation&quot;&gt;&lt;a role=&quot;tab&quot; href=&quot;#tab-panel-4189&quot; id=&quot;tab-4189&quot; aria-selected=&quot;true&quot; tabindex=&quot;0&quot;&gt;npm&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li role=&quot;presentation&quot;&gt;&lt;a role=&quot;tab&quot; href=&quot;#tab-panel-4190&quot; id=&quot;tab-4190&quot; aria-selected=&quot;false&quot; tabindex=&quot;-1&quot;&gt;yarn&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li role=&quot;presentation&quot;&gt;&lt;a role=&quot;tab&quot; href=&quot;#tab-panel-4191&quot; id=&quot;tab-4191&quot; aria-selected=&quot;false&quot; tabindex=&quot;-1&quot;&gt;pnpm&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li role=&quot;presentation&quot;&gt;&lt;a role=&quot;tab&quot; href=&quot;#tab-panel-4192&quot; id=&quot;tab-4192&quot; aria-selected=&quot;false&quot; tabindex=&quot;-1&quot;&gt;cargo&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;tab-panel-4189&quot; aria-labelledby=&quot;tab-4189&quot; role=&quot;tabpanel&quot;&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;npm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;install&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/cli@next&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;npm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;run&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;migrate&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;tab-panel-4190&quot; aria-labelledby=&quot;tab-4190&quot; role=&quot;tabpanel&quot; hidden&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;yarn&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;upgrade&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/cli@next&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;yarn&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;migrate&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;tab-panel-4191&quot; aria-labelledby=&quot;tab-4191&quot; role=&quot;tabpanel&quot; hidden&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;pnpm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;update&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/cli@next&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;pnpm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;migrate&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;tab-panel-4192&quot; aria-labelledby=&quot;tab-4192&quot; role=&quot;tabpanel&quot; hidden&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;cargo&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;install&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri-cli&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--version&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;^2.0.0-rc&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--locked&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;cargo&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;migrate&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;starlight-tabs-restore&gt;&lt;/starlight-tabs-restore&gt;&lt;/starlight-tabs&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/aside&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Otherwise please read the detailed section &lt;a href=&quot;#tauri-core-plugins&quot;&gt;below&lt;/a&gt; explaining the changes and how to manually migrate.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;For downstream consumers of Tauri as a library or app developers fiddling with the internals of Tauri we have a bigger &lt;a href=&quot;#rust-api-surface-refactor&quot;&gt;refactor&lt;/a&gt; you should check out.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;tauri-core-plugins&quot;&gt;Tauri Core Plugins&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;With Tauri 2.0 we migrated most of the 1.x core functionality into separate plugins, which allows us to iterate on these independently of Tauri’s core and lowers the barrier for first contributors on functionality.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;This migration also included keeping some functionality inside Tauri as pseudo plugins. Fully qualified plugins need to implement the &lt;a href=&quot;https://docs.rs/tauri/2.0.0-rc/tauri/plugin/trait.Plugin.html&quot;&gt;&lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;Plugin Trait&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/a&gt; and need to be individual crates following the &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri-plugin-&amp;#x3C;plugin name&gt;&lt;/code&gt; naming scheme. For core plugins, the second condition was not possible as we would have circular dependencies on Tauri. So we created pseudo plugins, which are always initialized by Tauri itself and only implement the plugin trait. These are for example &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;window&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;path&lt;/code&gt; or &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;webview&lt;/code&gt;. Right now these are allowed in the &lt;a href=&quot;https://v2.tauri.app/security/capabilities/&quot;&gt;capabilities&lt;/a&gt; of your Tauri application in the following way:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;...&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;permissions&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;: [&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;path:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;event:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;window:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;app:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;image:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;resources:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;menu:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tray:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;]&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;...&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;This has multiple problems:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Any plugin crate which has a colliding name will break our build process (e.g. &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri-plugin-window&lt;/code&gt; crate) and our cli for adding plugins (e.g. &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;cargo tauri add window&lt;/code&gt;)&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;We can’t use any core pseudo plugin naming that is already used by existing plugins (e.g. if we would ever want to create&lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri-plugin-mobile-core&lt;/code&gt; and it is already used we will encounter the first problem)&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;It is unclear for developers what is a core plugin and what is a dedicated plugin when looking at capabilities&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Our approach is to use a fixed namespace for core plugins, which is documented and enforced by the Tauri core. All plugins starting with &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;core:&lt;/code&gt; or the plugin name &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;core&lt;/code&gt; are now considered core pseudo plugins and will only be initialized if they are in the Tauri codebase.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;This will cause a breaking change to all capabilites enabling Tauri core features. The above example will be changed to look like this:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;...&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;permissions&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;: [&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;core:path:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;core:event:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;core:window:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;core:app:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;core:image:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;core:resources:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;core:menu:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;core:tray:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;]&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;...&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We also added a new special &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;core:default&lt;/code&gt; permission set which will contain all default permissions of all core plugins, so you can simplify the permissions boilerplate in your capabilities config.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;...&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;permissions&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;: [&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;core:default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;]&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;...&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We consider the core default exposure to be reasonably secure and safe to enable by default, with limited impact in case of a compromised frontend.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;To migrate from the latest beta version you need to prepend all core permission identifiers in your capabilities with &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;core:&lt;/code&gt; or switch to the &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;core:default&lt;/code&gt; permission and remove old core plugin identifiers.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;development-server-for-mobile&quot;&gt;Development Server for Mobile&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We introduced changes to the network exposure of the built-in development server &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/pull/10437&quot;&gt;PR #10437&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/pull/10456&quot;&gt;PR #10456&lt;/a&gt;.
With the changes shipped in the 2.0.0-rc.0 release of the Tauri CLI, we can connect to your development server running on localhost when targetting
Android and iOS (previously this was only possible when developing a desktop application).&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;This means you no longer need to expose your development server on the public network.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;aside aria-label=&quot;Note&quot;&gt;&lt;p aria-hidden=&quot;true&quot;&gt;Note&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;p&gt;When running your app on a physical iOS device we actually need to bind the development server
on a TUN address provided by the device.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;This kind of connection is currently only possible when Xcode is opened and connected to your device,
so we do not use this interface by default - we have to bind your development server to your public network address
while we find out a way to connect to the device ourselves.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;To use the iOS device’s address instead of the public network, run &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri ios dev --force-ip-prompt&lt;/code&gt;
to select the iOS device’s TUN address (ends with &lt;strong&gt;::2&lt;/strong&gt;).&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;The IP address your frontend must listen to is provided by the &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;TAURI_DEV_HOST&lt;/code&gt; environment variable.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;p&gt;Here’s an example of a Vite configuration migration:&lt;/p&gt;&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;2.0.0-beta:&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;import&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; { defineConfig } &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;from&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;vite&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;import&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; { svelte } &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;from&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@sveltejs/vite-plugin-svelte&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;import&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; { internalIpV4Sync } &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;from&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;internal-ip&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;const &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;mobile&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; = !!&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;/&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;android&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;|&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;ios&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;/&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;exec&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;process&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;env&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;TAURI_ENV_PLATFORM&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;);&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;export&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;defineConfig&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;({&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;plugins: [&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;svelte&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;()],&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;clearScreen: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;false&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;server: {&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;host: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;mobile&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;?&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;0.0.0.0&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;:&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;false&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;port: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;1420&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;strictPort: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;true&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;hmr: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;mobile&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;      &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;?&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; {&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;          &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;protocol: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;ws&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;          &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;host: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;internalIpV4Sync&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(),&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;          &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;port: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;1421&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;        &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;      &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;:&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;undefined&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;},&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;});&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;2.0.0-rc:&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;import&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; { defineConfig } &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;from&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;vite&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;import&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; Unocss &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;from&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;unocss/vite&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;import&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; { svelte } &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;from&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@sveltejs/vite-plugin-svelte&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;const &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;host&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; = &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;process&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;env&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;TAURI_DEV_HOST&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;export&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;default&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;defineConfig&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;({&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;plugins: [&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;svelte&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;()],&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;clearScreen: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;false&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;server: {&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;host: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;host&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;||&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;false&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;port: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;1420&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;strictPort: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;true&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;hmr: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;host&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;      &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;?&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; {&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;          &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;protocol: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;ws&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;          &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;host: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;host&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;          &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;port: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;1430&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;        &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;      &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;:&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;undefined&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;},&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;});&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/aside&gt;&lt;aside aria-label=&quot;Note&quot;&gt;&lt;p aria-hidden=&quot;true&quot;&gt;Note&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;p&gt;This means the &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;internal-ip&lt;/code&gt; NPM package is no longer required, you can directly use the TAURI_DEV_HOST value instead.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/aside&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;rust-api-surface-refactor&quot;&gt;Rust API Surface Refactor&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;With a coordinated effort between multiple working group members we partially changed our Rust API exposure. This affects only consumers of our Rust API and should have no breaking change impact for Tauri application developers.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;This was motivated by a recent security advisory &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/security/advisories/GHSA-57fm-592m-34r7&quot;&gt;CVE-2024-35222&lt;/a&gt;, as the fix needed to introduce additional fields to a structure that was directly publicly exposed and caused breaking changes to some projects and internal usage.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We concluded that this overexposure will hinder us in the future and will cause unnecessary breaking changes, so we decided that going from beta to RC will be the last chance for us to implement this until we start down the road of Tauri 3.0.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We reduced the amount of publicly exposed components, which are meant for internal use. Additionally, we made our publicly exposed structures either non-exhaustive or transformed them into exposing builder patterns or constructors. In some cases we added a new &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;extend&lt;/code&gt; field to allow dynamic additions in the future. Finally, we made sure to document which modules of Tauri are considered unstable.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;This will help us to provide (security) fixes or changes without breaking interfaces that are considered stable.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Please take a closer look at the introduced and discussed changes in the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/pull/10158&quot;&gt;#10158&lt;/a&gt; pull request.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;external-security-audit&quot;&gt;External Security Audit&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;aside aria-label=&quot;TL;DR&quot;&gt;&lt;p aria-hidden=&quot;true&quot;&gt;TL;DR&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;p&gt;The audit concluded some time ago and all issues were fixed and retested. Report is &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/blob/dev/audits/Radically_Open_Security-v2-report.pdf&quot;&gt;here&lt;/a&gt;. Please upgrade to the release candidate to ensure fixes for the reported issues.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/aside&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We have been quiet on this front for some time as we have been busy fixing and discussing issues discovered during the beta versions.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We never marketed version 2 beta releases as production ready but were aware of some apps deployed into production. This caused us to announce and distribute a security patch for one of the findings (&lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/security/advisories/GHSA-57fm-592m-34r7&quot;&gt;CVE-2024-35222&lt;/a&gt;) which was also independently discovered by a Tauri community member.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;All other findings were fixed in multiple beta versions but we did not create advisories for these. We concluded a full heads up could wait until the release candidate, as the findings mainly affect the development phase or have no critical severity.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;With the release candidate we will add the full report to our repository. Please take your time to read the report and learn more about the awesome work of &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/gronke&quot;&gt;@gronke&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/pcwizz&quot;&gt;@pcwizz&lt;/a&gt; from &lt;a href=&quot;https://www.radicallyopensecurity.com/&quot;&gt;RadicallyOpenSecurity&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The whole audit was funded by the great folks at &lt;a href=&quot;https://nlnet.nl/&quot;&gt;NLNet&lt;/a&gt; and we are super grateful to be in the privileged position to get fully funded external security audits.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;call-to-action&quot;&gt;Call to Action&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;All of the above topics share a common theme. These would not have been possible without the continuous support of the community, our working group and other movements working towards improving the status quo.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Before we are going to release Tauri 2.0 we want to make sure that your voices are heard, your PRs are acknowledged and the documentation is helpful for &lt;strong&gt;YOU&lt;/strong&gt; so that you can build the next generation of cross platform apps.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Currently we have over 30 people in our working group on Github but even more involved in our &lt;a href=&quot;https://discord.gg/tauri&quot;&gt;Discord&lt;/a&gt;. These awesome people are mostly working on Tauri in their free time with very few exceptions. We currently see a number of issues, PRs and discussions being unsolved and open for longer than we would like to.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;To improve this situation we ask &lt;strong&gt;YOU&lt;/strong&gt; to get involved into the Tauri project.We have all kinds of situations where we are able to accept event the tiniest contribution.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;If you are familiar with Tauri and have used it already during your journey, please take your time to check out the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/discussions&quot;&gt;Github Discussions&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/issues&quot;&gt;Github Issues&lt;/a&gt; and our &lt;a href=&quot;https://discord.gg/tauri&quot;&gt;Discord Support&lt;/a&gt;. Maybe you have already solved the issues your fellow newcomers to Tauri are experiencing right now.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;If you think that some of these problems you have seen are generic and should be documented somewhere we probably have the perfect place for it in our official &lt;a href=&quot;https://v2.tauri.app&quot;&gt;documentation&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;To contribute improvements or additions we are open for PRs in the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri-docs&quot;&gt;tauri-docs&lt;/a&gt; repository. Please make sure you’ve read the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri-docs/blob/v2/.github/CONTRIBUTING.md&quot;&gt;guidelines&lt;/a&gt; for contribution though.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;If you are in the position to understand and translate the current documentation into your native language we appreciate &lt;a href=&quot;https://v2.tauri.app/contribute/translate-status&quot;&gt;content translations&lt;/a&gt; to our documentation.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;If you have followed our project for a while but never made a contribution we would be happy to understand what has prevented you from doing so and how we could improve this. Please reach out to us in our &lt;a href=&quot;https://discord.gg/tauri&quot;&gt;Discord&lt;/a&gt; or in our &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/discussions&quot;&gt;Github Discussions&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;too-long-didnt-read&quot;&gt;Too Long Didn’t Read&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Tauri 2.0 Release Candidate out now!&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Some migration from beta is needed. Check out &lt;a href=&quot;https://v2.tauri.app/start/migrate/from-tauri-1/#automated-migration&quot;&gt;&lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri migrate&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;External Security Audit for 2.0 is available &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/blob/dev/audits/Radically_Open_Security-v2-report.pdf&quot;&gt;here&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;All findings are fixed and fixes were verified&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Documentation is our focus until stable release&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Tauri is looking for more contributors and community involvement&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;</content:encoded></item><item><title>Announcing Tauri 1.7.0</title><link>https://v2.tauri.app/blog/tauri-1-7/</link><guid isPermaLink="true">https://v2.tauri.app/blog/tauri-1-7/</guid><description>The Tauri team is happy to announce the 1.7.0 release.</description><pubDate>Mon, 01 Jul 2024 00:00:00 GMT</pubDate><content:encoded>&lt;p&gt;The Tauri team is happy to announce the 1.7 release. This version includes several bug fixes, performance improvements and features backported from the upcoming v2 release.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;upgrading&quot;&gt;Upgrading&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Make sure to update both NPM and Cargo dependencies to the 1.7.0 release. You can update the dependencies with:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;starlight-tabs data-sync-key=&quot;cmd&quot;&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;ul role=&quot;tablist&quot;&gt;&lt;li role=&quot;presentation&quot;&gt;&lt;a role=&quot;tab&quot; href=&quot;#tab-panel-4193&quot; id=&quot;tab-4193&quot; aria-selected=&quot;true&quot; tabindex=&quot;0&quot;&gt;npm&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li role=&quot;presentation&quot;&gt;&lt;a role=&quot;tab&quot; href=&quot;#tab-panel-4194&quot; id=&quot;tab-4194&quot; aria-selected=&quot;false&quot; tabindex=&quot;-1&quot;&gt;yarn&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li role=&quot;presentation&quot;&gt;&lt;a role=&quot;tab&quot; href=&quot;#tab-panel-4195&quot; id=&quot;tab-4195&quot; aria-selected=&quot;false&quot; tabindex=&quot;-1&quot;&gt;pnpm&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li role=&quot;presentation&quot;&gt;&lt;a role=&quot;tab&quot; href=&quot;#tab-panel-4196&quot; id=&quot;tab-4196&quot; aria-selected=&quot;false&quot; tabindex=&quot;-1&quot;&gt;cargo&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;tab-panel-4193&quot; aria-labelledby=&quot;tab-4193&quot; role=&quot;tabpanel&quot;&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;npm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;install&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/cli@latest&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/api@latest&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;tab-panel-4194&quot; aria-labelledby=&quot;tab-4194&quot; role=&quot;tabpanel&quot; hidden&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;yarn&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;upgrade&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/cli&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/api&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--latest&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;tab-panel-4195&quot; aria-labelledby=&quot;tab-4195&quot; role=&quot;tabpanel&quot; hidden&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;pnpm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;update&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/cli&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/api&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--latest&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;tab-panel-4196&quot; aria-labelledby=&quot;tab-4196&quot; role=&quot;tabpanel&quot; hidden&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;cargo&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;update&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;starlight-tabs-restore&gt;&lt;/starlight-tabs-restore&gt;&lt;/starlight-tabs&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;whats-in-170&quot;&gt;What’s in 1.7.0&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;shell-api-performance-improvement&quot;&gt;Shell API performance improvement&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The shell’s &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;Command::execute&lt;/code&gt; API has been optimized to only use the IPC a single time instead of streaming data, improving usage of verbose shell scripts.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;feature-backport-from-v2&quot;&gt;Feature backport from v2&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Thanks to community effort we have backported a few bundler features from v2 into the v1 release.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h4 id=&quot;custom-windows-codesign-script&quot;&gt;Custom Windows codesign script&lt;/h4&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;By default the Windows packaging uses &lt;a href=&quot;https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/seccrypto/signtool&quot;&gt;SignTool&lt;/a&gt;, which only works on Windows so it’s not useful when cross compiling.
In this release we have backported the custom sign command feature, which allows using &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;osslsigncode&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;relic&lt;/code&gt; and other alternatives that can run on Unix systems and support hardware tokens, Azure Key Vault and more.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h4 id=&quot;rpm-bundle&quot;&gt;RPM bundle&lt;/h4&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;RPM packaging have been available to Tauri v2 users for a while, and it is now also available on the v1 channel.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;dmg-configuration&quot;&gt;DMG configuration&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;DMG installers are now configurable: you can change the position of the icons and the window size to fit better within a custom background.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;other-changes&quot;&gt;Other changes&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Check out the entire list of changes:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/releases/tag/tauri-v1.7.0&quot;&gt;tauri&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/releases/tag/tauri-cli-v1.6.0&quot;&gt;tauri-cli&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/releases/tag/tauri-bundler-v1.6.0&quot;&gt;tauri-bundler&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;</content:encoded></item><item><title>Tauri Board Elections 2024</title><link>https://v2.tauri.app/blog/tauri-board-elections-2024/</link><guid isPermaLink="true">https://v2.tauri.app/blog/tauri-board-elections-2024/</guid><description>The Tauri Programme is celebrating its third anniversary of Tauri becoming a programme within The Commons Conservancy. We are hard at work bringing v2 to a stable release, and now the next round of Tauri Board Director elections is upon us!</description><pubDate>Fri, 28 Jun 2024 00:00:00 GMT</pubDate><content:encoded>&lt;p&gt;The Tauri Programme is celebrating its third anniversary of Tauri becoming a programme within &lt;a href=&quot;https://commonsconservancy.org/&quot;&gt;The Commons Conservancy&lt;/a&gt;. We are hard at work bringing v2 to a stable release, and now the next round of Tauri Board Director elections is upon us! Want to &lt;a href=&quot;#interested-in-getting-involved&quot;&gt;get involved in other ways&lt;/a&gt; and help Tauri towards v2? We would love your contributions especially to help evolve the documentation!&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;board-elections&quot;&gt;Board Elections&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The Tauri Board of Directors is the central decision-making body for the Tauri Programme and is responsible for its overall health and stability. Additional details can be found on the &lt;a href=&quot;https://v2.tauri.app/about/governance/&quot;&gt;Tauri Governance page&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;In order to provide continuity from one year to another, the elections of seats are staggered over the course of 2 years. This means that one year a portion of the seats are elected, and the following year the remaining seats are elected. A Tauri Director seat is an elected position with a term of 2 years. The Board has a minimum of 3 Directors and a maximum of 7. This year there are 2 seats open for election.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;applying-for-candidacy&quot;&gt;Applying for Candidacy&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;There are 3 steps to express interest and apply for candidacy:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;ol&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Learn about the role of a Tauri Board Director on the &lt;a href=&quot;https://v2.tauri.app/about/governance/#tauri-board--board-directors&quot;&gt;Governance page&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Prepare a written introduction about yourself that covers who you are, your history and relevance for Tauri, and what you would like to bring to the Board.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Apply prior to July 5th, 2024&lt;/strong&gt; by emailing &lt;a href=&quot;mailto:board@tauri.app&quot;&gt;board@tauri.app&lt;/a&gt;, messaging the &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;@board&lt;/code&gt; role on the &lt;a href=&quot;https://discord.com/invite/tauri&quot;&gt;Tauri Discord&lt;/a&gt;, or by directly messaging Jacob Bolda on Discord (&lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;@jacobbolda&lt;/code&gt;) or email (&lt;a href=&quot;mailto:jacob@tauri.app&quot;&gt;jacob@tauri.app&lt;/a&gt;).&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ol&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Voting will take place starting on July 7th through July 12th, 2024 where Tauri Working Group Members will cast their votes. We’re expecting to announce the results on or before July 16, 2024. If elected, we’ll ask you to sign &lt;a href=&quot;https://dracc.commonsconservancy.org/0016/&quot;&gt;this pledge&lt;/a&gt; if you haven’t done so previously.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;If you have any questions or would like more information please reach out by emailing &lt;a href=&quot;mailto:board@tauri.app&quot;&gt;board@tauri.app&lt;/a&gt;, messaging the &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;@board&lt;/code&gt; role on the &lt;a href=&quot;https://discord.com/invite/tauri&quot;&gt;Tauri Discord&lt;/a&gt;, or by directly messaging Jacob Bolda on Discord (&lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;@jacobbolda&lt;/code&gt;) or email (&lt;a href=&quot;mailto:jacob@tauri.app&quot;&gt;jacob@tauri.app&lt;/a&gt;).&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;interested-in-getting-involved&quot;&gt;Interested In Getting Involved?&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;If joining the Board isn’t for you, we are always looking to enable involvement at any level within Tauri. The documentation for v2 is a large focus of the remaining effort. See the issues within the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri-docs&quot;&gt;tauri-apps/tauri-docs repo&lt;/a&gt;. Additionally reach out and follow along within Discord. Add a &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;@notify-*&lt;/code&gt; role to stay apprised of news and questions from the team, or follow along and ask questions in the &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;#dev&lt;/code&gt; or &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;#docs&lt;/code&gt; channels under the &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;CONTRIBUTORS&lt;/code&gt; section.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Prepared for some more responsibility? A Domain in the Working Group represents a specific area of interest.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Development&lt;/strong&gt;: Developing and maintaining the Tauri software&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Community&lt;/strong&gt;: Looks after the broader community resources and the public presence of Tauri&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Governance &amp;#x26; Guidance&lt;/strong&gt;: Involved with all things organizational in nature&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Operations&lt;/strong&gt;: Responsible for all the tooling and infrastructure Tauri needs to get work done&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;p&gt;This gives clear entry points for a new member of the Working Group to jump into right away. It also allows us to continue growing our core competency of core Tauri development while giving additional focus to all areas which support the Tauri organization as a whole. Each of these domains will be represented by Domain Members and Domain Leads, but membership is not mutually exclusive.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;All domains within Tauri are equally important to providing the best experience possible, as well as the wonderful support of the community. ❤️&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;hr&gt;
&lt;p&gt;With your help, we can continue to improve and catalyze the growth and sustainability of the organization. You can learn more in the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/governance-and-guidance&quot;&gt;Governance and Guidance repo on GitHub&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
</content:encoded></item><item><title>Rust Security Advisory CVE-2024-24576</title><link>https://v2.tauri.app/blog/cve-2024-24576/</link><guid isPermaLink="true">https://v2.tauri.app/blog/cve-2024-24576/</guid><description>Recent Rust Security Advisory CVE-2024-24576.</description><pubDate>Wed, 10 Apr 2024 00:00:00 GMT</pubDate><content:encoded>&lt;p&gt;The Rust Security Response WG announced &lt;a href=&quot;https://blog.rust-lang.org/2024/04/09/cve-2024-24576.html&quot;&gt;&lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;CVE-2024-24576&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/a&gt;, which affects the Rust Standard Library on Windows.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;blockquote&gt;
&lt;p&gt;TL;DR: Upgrade your Rust version to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;1.77.2&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;/blockquote&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;how-does-it-affect-tauri-as-a-library&quot;&gt;How Does it Affect Tauri as a Library?&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Some Tauri organization repositories use batch files (&lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;cmd.exe&lt;/code&gt; under the hood) for developer environment tooling such as build scripts.
No reviewed repositories use batch files for runtime code.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We don’t see additional risks for the Tauri project based on this CVE.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Nevertheless, we will update our CI systems to use the latest Rust version.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;is-my-tauri-app-affected&quot;&gt;Is My Tauri App Affected?&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;In general you are possibly affected if you fulfil &lt;strong&gt;all&lt;/strong&gt; of the below criteria:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;You ship your app on &lt;strong&gt;Windows&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Your project enables the Tauri v1 &lt;a href=&quot;https://tauri.app/v1/api/js/shell/&quot;&gt;&lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;shell&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/a&gt; feature with &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;&quot;execute&quot;: true&lt;/code&gt; or the v2 &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/plugins-workspace/tree/v2/plugins/shell&quot;&gt;&lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;shell-plugin&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/a&gt; with &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;allow-execute&lt;/code&gt; permission&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;You allow arguments in the &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;scope&lt;/code&gt; element of the &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;shell&lt;/code&gt; feature&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;You pass untrusted input to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;cmd.exe&lt;/code&gt; or &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;.bat&lt;/code&gt;/&lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;.cmd&lt;/code&gt; files and improperly validate the scope&lt;/strong&gt; (🚩)&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;p&gt;If any of these criteria are not fulfilled in your application you are likely &lt;strong&gt;NOT&lt;/strong&gt; affected.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;If you implement custom commands or logic written in your application that directly exposes the Rust &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;Command&lt;/code&gt; with arguments provided at runtime, you may be affected.
While not Tauri specific, this pattern could affect any Rust project.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;conclusion&quot;&gt;Conclusion&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Please upgrade your Rust version to &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;1.77.2&lt;/code&gt;
as soon as possible and distribute updates to your users.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;This investigation and writeup was performed in cooperation with our partner &lt;a href=&quot;https://crabnebula.dev/blog/cve-2024-24576/&quot;&gt;CrabNebula&lt;/a&gt; ❤️.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;hr&gt;
&lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://flatt.tech/research/posts/batbadbut-you-cant-securely-execute-commands-on-windows/&quot;&gt;Read more about this security advisory here&lt;/a&gt;.
This affects many programming languages, this specific CVE is just the one filed for Rust.&lt;/p&gt;
</content:encoded></item><item><title>Announcing Tauri 1.6.0</title><link>https://v2.tauri.app/blog/tauri-1-6/</link><guid isPermaLink="true">https://v2.tauri.app/blog/tauri-1-6/</guid><description>The Tauri team is happy to announce the 1.6.0 release.</description><pubDate>Mon, 19 Feb 2024 00:00:00 GMT</pubDate><content:encoded>&lt;p&gt;The Tauri team is happy to announce the 1.6 release. This version includes several new features and important bug fixes such as improved code signing on macOS, updater enhancements and an event loop crash on all platforms.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;upgrading&quot;&gt;Upgrading&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Make sure to update both NPM and Cargo dependencies to the 1.6.0 release. You can update the dependencies with:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;starlight-tabs data-sync-key=&quot;cmd&quot;&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;ul role=&quot;tablist&quot;&gt;&lt;li role=&quot;presentation&quot;&gt;&lt;a role=&quot;tab&quot; href=&quot;#tab-panel-4197&quot; id=&quot;tab-4197&quot; aria-selected=&quot;true&quot; tabindex=&quot;0&quot;&gt;npm&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li role=&quot;presentation&quot;&gt;&lt;a role=&quot;tab&quot; href=&quot;#tab-panel-4198&quot; id=&quot;tab-4198&quot; aria-selected=&quot;false&quot; tabindex=&quot;-1&quot;&gt;yarn&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li role=&quot;presentation&quot;&gt;&lt;a role=&quot;tab&quot; href=&quot;#tab-panel-4199&quot; id=&quot;tab-4199&quot; aria-selected=&quot;false&quot; tabindex=&quot;-1&quot;&gt;pnpm&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;li role=&quot;presentation&quot;&gt;&lt;a role=&quot;tab&quot; href=&quot;#tab-panel-4200&quot; id=&quot;tab-4200&quot; aria-selected=&quot;false&quot; tabindex=&quot;-1&quot;&gt;cargo&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;tab-panel-4197&quot; aria-labelledby=&quot;tab-4197&quot; role=&quot;tabpanel&quot;&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;npm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;install&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/cli@latest&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/api@latest&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;tab-panel-4198&quot; aria-labelledby=&quot;tab-4198&quot; role=&quot;tabpanel&quot; hidden&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;yarn&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;upgrade&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/cli&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/api&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--latest&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;tab-panel-4199&quot; aria-labelledby=&quot;tab-4199&quot; role=&quot;tabpanel&quot; hidden&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;pnpm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;update&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/cli&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/api&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--latest&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id=&quot;tab-panel-4200&quot; aria-labelledby=&quot;tab-4200&quot; role=&quot;tabpanel&quot; hidden&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;cargo&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;update&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;starlight-tabs-restore&gt;&lt;/starlight-tabs-restore&gt;&lt;/starlight-tabs&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;whats-in-160&quot;&gt;What’s in 1.6.0&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;event-loop-crash&quot;&gt;Event loop crash&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We finally got a good stack trace and got a fix for a long standing crash on all platforms.
This crash was a challenge to fix since it only happened when the application is running for a long time,
so we thank everyone that made sure the fix works.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;code-signing-improvements&quot;&gt;Code signing improvements&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Tauri now detects nested dylib, app, xpc and frameworks inside your macOS app bundle and codesigns each of them. This ensures your app can use some external libraries and be codesigned and notarized.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;updater-enhancement&quot;&gt;Updater enhancement&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The auto updater now keeps the command line arguments on Windows.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;other-changes&quot;&gt;Other changes&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Check out the entire list of changes:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/releases/tag/tauri-v1.6.0&quot;&gt;tauri&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/releases/tag/tauri-cli-v1.5.10&quot;&gt;tauri-cli&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/releases/tag/tauri-bundler-v1.5.0&quot;&gt;tauri-bundler&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;</content:encoded></item><item><title>Announcing the Tauri v2 Beta Release</title><link>https://v2.tauri.app/blog/tauri-2-0-0-beta/</link><guid isPermaLink="true">https://v2.tauri.app/blog/tauri-2-0-0-beta/</guid><description>Tauri v2 is ready to take the next step towards being stable! 2.0.0-beta.0 has been published.</description><pubDate>Fri, 02 Feb 2024 00:00:00 GMT</pubDate><content:encoded>&lt;p&gt;&lt;img alt=&quot;Hero Image&quot; loading=&quot;lazy&quot; decoding=&quot;async&quot; width=&quot;1280&quot; height=&quot;640&quot; src=&quot;https://v2.tauri.app/_astro/header.DJC8YrJ3_rv4kd.webp&quot; srcset=&quot;&quot;&gt;&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Tauri v2 has been in progress for &lt;a href=&quot;https://v2.tauri.app/blog/tauri-mobile-alpha/&quot;&gt;over a year&lt;/a&gt; and it is now ready to take the next step towards being stable! We have just released &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/releases/tag/tauri-v2.0.0-beta.0&quot;&gt;Tauri v2.0.0-beta.0&lt;/a&gt; which represents a major milestone from our &lt;a href=&quot;https://v2.tauri.app/blog/roadmap-to-tauri-2-0/#beta&quot;&gt;roadmap&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The v2 release introduces mobile support to Tauri and also comes with several new features that have been requested by the community. Let’s get a high level overview of the major changes:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;mobile&quot;&gt;Mobile&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Developing an application for desktop and mobile has never been easier. Tauri v2 is a huge statement on cross platform development now that we support Android and iOS. You can bring your existing desktop implementation and seamlessly port it to mobile, with access to native APIs and the great developer experience of the Tauri CLI.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;permissions&quot;&gt;Permissions&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The v1 allowlist is a good tool for securing your frontend from accessing unnecessary APIs, but its configuration is not fine grained and it lacks multiwindow support. The 2.0.0-beta.0 release includes a new approach for command access based on Access Control List. It is now possible to allow commands and define scopes for specific windows or even remote URLs.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;new-features&quot;&gt;New Features&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;v2 includes many of the most requested features by the Tauri community:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;revamped-ipc&quot;&gt;Revamped IPC&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The v1 Inter-Process Communication (IPC) which is responsible for delivering messages between the Rust and JavaScript layers uses a very rudimentary webview interface which forces us to serialize all messages to strings and is super slow to deliver responses. The new v2 IPC uses custom protocols, which is more reminiscent in function and performance to how the webview handles regular HTTP based communication, see the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/pull/7170#issuecomment-1583279023&quot;&gt;pull request&lt;/a&gt; for more information.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Additionally, there is a new channel API so you can quickly send data from Rust to your frontend.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;multiwebview&quot;&gt;Multiwebview&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Tauri now supports adding multiple webviews to a single window. This is also a highly anticipated &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/issues/2709&quot;&gt;feature request&lt;/a&gt;.
Note this is still an unfinished feature that is hidden behind an &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;unstable&lt;/code&gt; Cargo feature flag while we review the API design together with the community.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;menu-and-tray-icon-javascript-apis&quot;&gt;Menu and tray icon JavaScript APIs&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Previously you could only configure window menus and tray icons via Rust code. Now you can do so on the JavaScript side too, which is a lot easier! We also added APIs to manage the macOS application menu specifically.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;context-menus&quot;&gt;Context Menus&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
