       &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;ret.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;put&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;value&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;, &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;value&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;        &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;invoke.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;resolve&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(ret)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;iOS plugin:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;span&gt;ExamplePlugin.swift&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;import&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; UIKit&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;import&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; WebKit&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;import&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; Tauri&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;class&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; ExamplePlugin: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Plugin &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;{&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@objc&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;public&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;func&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;ping&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;_&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;invoke&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;: Invoke&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;throws&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; {&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;let&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; value &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;=&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; invoke.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;getString&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;value&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;invoke.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;resolve&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;[&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;value&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;:&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; value &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;as&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Any&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;]&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;@_cdecl&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;init_plugin_example&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;func&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;initPlugin&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;name&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;: SRString, &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;webview&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;: WKWebView&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;?&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; {&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Tauri.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;registerPlugin&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;webview&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;: webview, &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;name&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;: name.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;toString&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;()&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;, &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;plugin&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;ExamplePlugin&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;())&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Rust code to initialize the plugin:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;use&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; tauri&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;::&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;{&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;plugin&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;::&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;{Builder, TauriPlugin},&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Manager, Runtime,&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;};&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;#[cfg(target_os &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;=&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;ios&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;)]&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;::&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;ios_plugin_binding!&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;init_plugin_example&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;);&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;pub&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;fn&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;init&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&amp;#x3C;R&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;:&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; Runtime&gt;() &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;-&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; TauriPlugin&amp;#x3C;R&gt; {&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Builder&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;::&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;new&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;example&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;setup&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;|&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;app&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;, &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;api&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;|&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; {&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;      &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;#[cfg(target_os &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;=&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;android&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;)]&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;      &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;api&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;register_android_plugin&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;com.plugin.example&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;, &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;ExamplePlugin&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;?&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;      &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;#[cfg(target_os &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;=&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;ios&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;)]&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;      &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;api&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;register_ios_plugin&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;init_plugin_example&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;?&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;      &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Ok(())&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;})&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;    &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;build&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;()&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;}&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Frontend code to call the plugin command:&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;import&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; { invoke } &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;from&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;@tauri-apps/api/tauri&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;invoke&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;plugin:example|ping&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;, { value: &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Tauri&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; })&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;then&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;{ &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;value&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; }&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;=&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;console&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;.&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;log&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Response&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&apos;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;, &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;value&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;);&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Check out the upcoming &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/plugins-workspace/pull/260&quot;&gt;camera plugin&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/pull/6339&quot;&gt;path plugin&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;</content:encoded></item><item><title>create-tauri-app Version 3 Released</title><link>https://v2.tauri.app/blog/create-tauri-app-version-3-released/</link><guid isPermaLink="true">https://v2.tauri.app/blog/create-tauri-app-version-3-released/</guid><description>create-tauri-app Version 3 is released with support for alpha versions of Tauri, mobile project support and DX improvements</description><pubDate>Wed, 01 Mar 2023 00:00:00 GMT</pubDate><content:encoded>&lt;p&gt;&lt;img alt=&quot;hero image&quot; loading=&quot;lazy&quot; decoding=&quot;async&quot; width=&quot;1280&quot; height=&quot;640&quot; src=&quot;https://v2.tauri.app/_astro/header.cyPt1LbD_1AWOnN.webp&quot; srcset=&quot;&quot;&gt;&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;A new major version of &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/create-tauri-app&quot;&gt;create-tauri-app&lt;/a&gt; has been released. This version adds support for the alpha version of Tauri 2.0, the ability to initialize iOS and Android projects and removes some less commonly used templates to make the project more maintainable.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;tauri-20-alpha--mobile-support&quot;&gt;Tauri 2.0 Alpha &amp;#x26; Mobile Support&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The &lt;a href=&quot;https://tauri.app/blog/2022/12/09/tauri-mobile-alpha&quot;&gt;first alpha version of Tauri 2.0 was published almost 3 months ago&lt;/a&gt; which brought initial mobile support for Android and iOS. Previously there wasn’t an easy way to create a new project to test either the changes in the alpha version nor the mobile support. Well, that ends now!&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Starting with version 3 of &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/create-tauri-app&quot;&gt;create-tauri-app&lt;/a&gt;, you can now pass &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;--beta&lt;/code&gt; flag and it will bootstrap an app that uses &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri@2.0.0-beta&lt;/code&gt;. When adding the &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;--beta&lt;/code&gt; flag it will automatically prompt you if you’d like to add mobile support. You can also use the &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;--mobile&lt;/code&gt; flag to automatically make it mobile compatible.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;To get started:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;# pnpm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;pnpm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;create&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri-app&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--alpha&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;# yarn&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;yarn&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;create&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri-app&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--alpha&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;# npm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;npm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;create&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri-app&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--alpha&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;# Cargo&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;cargo&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;install&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;create-tauri-app&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--locked&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;cargo&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;create-tauri-app&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--alpha&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;# Bash&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;sh&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&amp;#x3C;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;curl&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; https://create.tauri.app/sh&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--alpha&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;# Powershell&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;$env&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;:&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;CTA_ARGS&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;=&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--alpha&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&quot;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;iwr&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;-useb&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;https://create.tauri.app/ps&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;|&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;iex&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;prompt-improvements&quot;&gt;Prompt Improvements&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;With version 2 of create-tauri-app we also focused on improving the flow and experience of the prompted questions.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Previously the second prompt would ask “Choose your package manager”. This was a bit vague and could leave some with unanswered questions:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;What is &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;cargo&lt;/code&gt;?&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Is it a new Node.js package manager?&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Why choose it over &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;pnpm&lt;/code&gt; or &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;yarn&lt;/code&gt;?&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;p&gt;This would be even more vague if we decided to add a new frontend language (such as a webassembly Golang web application). We added a prompt asking to choose the package manager to ask which language would you prefer. It looks something like this:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;? Choose which language to use for your frontend ›&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Rust&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;❯ TypeScript / JavaScript  (pnpm, yarn, npm)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;After choosing the frontend language create-tauri-app will then prompt for package manager to use if the language has multiple (such as Node.js with npm, yarn, and pnpm).&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Another prompt that we felt problematic was the template selection prompt. It contained a lot of templates to choose from and was only growing with time:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;✔ Choose your package manager · pnpm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;? Choose your UI template ›&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;vanilla&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;vanilla-ts&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;vue&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;❯ vue-ts&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;svelte&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;svelte-ts&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;react&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;react-ts&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;solid&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;solid-ts&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;next&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;next-ts&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;preact&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;preact-ts&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;angular&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;clojurescript&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;svelte-kit&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;svelte-kit-ts&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We decided to split this up into 2 prompts. The first will ask which UI template to use:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;✔ Choose your package manager · pnpm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;? Choose your UI template ›&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Vanilla&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;❯ Vue&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Svelte&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;React&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Solid&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Angular&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Next&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;SvelteKit&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;ClojureScript&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Preact&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;And then the second will then ask any additional choices that are specific to that template (such as choosing between TypeScript or JavaScript for Vue):&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;✔ Choose your package manager · pnpm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;✔ Choose your UI template · Vue - (https://vuejs.org)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;? Choose your UI flavor ›&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;❯ TypeScript&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;  &lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;JavaScript&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Even with these refinements the list of templates was still quite large and would only grow over time. The next thing to look at was the list of templates themselves.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;removing-templates&quot;&gt;Removing Templates&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;When create-tauri-app version 2 was launched it quickly received PRs to add additional templates beyond what it originally launched with. While it was exciting to receive so much support from the community, it did bring up a couple of new challenges:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;It made it almost impossible to fit them in a single prompt&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Maintaining all the templates was becoming more difficult&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;p&gt;It was a hard balance between showing how flexible Tauri is but also making sure that the project was maintainable and had the DX that we wanted.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We decided to focus on the most popular frontend frameworks and used guidance from community surveys like &lt;a href=&quot;https://2022.stateofjs.com/en-US/libraries/front-end-frameworks/&quot;&gt;The State of JS&lt;/a&gt; and our own Tauri community feedback. With this we removed the &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;next&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;next-ts&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;preact&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;preact-ts&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;clojurescript&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;svelte-kit&lt;/code&gt; and &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;svelte-kit-ts&lt;/code&gt; templates and also closed PRs which aimed to add more templates.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;community-maintained-templates--previous-versions&quot;&gt;Community-Maintained Templates &amp;#x26; Previous Versions&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We want to make sure that we offer a place for the Tauri community to provide their own templates for the frameworks they love. The templates section of &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/awesome-tauri#templates&quot;&gt;awesome-tauri&lt;/a&gt; is just the place for that. We invite the community to submit a PR to the awesome-tauri repo with their templates so that they can be shared with the Tauri community. We’ll also be creating a section on the Tauri website to highlight and showcase these community templates.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;If you’d still like to use the previous templates from version 2 of create-tauri-app they are still published to npm and crates.io. Here’s how you can use a previous version:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;# pnpm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;pnpm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;create&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri-app@2&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;# yarn&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;yarn&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;create&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri-app@2&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;# npm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;npm&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;create&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;tauri-app@2&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;# Cargo&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;cargo&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;install&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;create-tauri-app&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--version&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;2.8.0&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;--locked&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;cargo&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;create-tauri-app&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;# Bash&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;sh&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&amp;#x3C;(&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;span&gt;curl&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; https://create.tauri.app/v/2.8.0/sh&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;)&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;# Powershell&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;iwr&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;-useb&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;https://create.tauri.app/v/2.8.0/ps&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;|&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;iex&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;However, as time goes, these templates will become out of date as Tauri and the frontend frameworks move forward. We’d recommend you look at the community templates in the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/awesome-tauri&quot;&gt;awesome-tauri&lt;/a&gt; repo and welcome submissions if something is missing.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;hr&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We hope that these changes make it easier for the community to try out the alpha and beta versions of Tauri 2.0 and also make the process to create a new Tauri app easier for everyone! We’d love to hear your feedback and invite you to join the conversation on the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri-docs/discussions/1137&quot;&gt;GitHub Discussion for this post&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
</content:encoded></item><item><title>Tauri Community Growth &amp; Feedback</title><link>https://v2.tauri.app/blog/tauri-community-growth-and-feedback/</link><guid isPermaLink="true">https://v2.tauri.app/blog/tauri-community-growth-and-feedback/</guid><description>Tauri Community Survey results and search improvements</description><pubDate>Thu, 09 Feb 2023 00:00:00 GMT</pubDate><content:encoded>&lt;p&gt;&lt;img alt=&quot;Community growth and feedback hero image&quot; loading=&quot;lazy&quot; decoding=&quot;async&quot; width=&quot;1280&quot; height=&quot;640&quot; src=&quot;https://v2.tauri.app/_astro/header.B87fyYky_1W9Wj0.webp&quot; srcset=&quot;&quot;&gt;&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Tauri has had an amazing past year. We &lt;a href=&quot;https://tauri.app/blog/2022/06/19/tauri-1-0/&quot;&gt;launched Tauri 1.0&lt;/a&gt;, &lt;a href=&quot;https://tauri.app/blog/2022/12/09/tauri-mobile-alpha&quot;&gt;announced the alpha version of Tauri Mobile&lt;/a&gt; and gathered a lot of valuable feedback from our community and users in the 2022 Tauri Community Survey.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;tauri-community-survey-results&quot;&gt;Tauri Community Survey Results&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;This year, we had over 600 responses to the Tauri Community Survey (over 3x more than the previous survey’s responses). We’d like to extend a very special thank you to &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/wusyong&quot;&gt;Wu Yu Wei&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/dklassic&quot;&gt;DK Liao&lt;/a&gt; for translating the survey from English into Simplified and Traditional Chinese so that we could include more people from the community and their feedback in the survey. You can download the public data for the survey &lt;a href=&quot;https://tauri.app/tauri-community-survey-2022-data.csv&quot;&gt;here&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We’ve heard your feedback and have started a couple of projects to directly address it. The first one we’d like to talk about is search.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;search-improvements-on-tauriapp&quot;&gt;Search Improvements on Tauri.app&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Without searching, can you ever find something? And if your telescope is cracked, can you see the planets or stars? We know that our search lens was dusty and scratched; it felt sometimes like a roll of the dice, and some folks even &lt;a href=&quot;https://twitter.com/danielcroe/status/1619703406482059265&quot;&gt;resorted to using ChatGPT&lt;/a&gt; to find answers.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Search has been a project that we’ve taken a few runs at over the years, but we’ve also learned that it’s not quite as easy as it first seems. &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/yankeeinlondon&quot;&gt;Ken&lt;/a&gt; from the Tauri Working Group did inspirational work with the previous &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;tauri-search&lt;/code&gt; project. He created a lot of the foundation and research in the previous project for us to understand what goes into search.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;hr&gt;
&lt;p&gt;With the next iteration of search, we had three goals in mind:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;ol&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Keep it maintainable&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Keep it working&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Keep it open source&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ol&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;search-engine&quot;&gt;Search Engine&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;First, we needed to choose a backend search engine to store and serve the search index. When evaluating search engines, we wanted to make sure we used something that shared our values of open source software and a small footprint. After comparing and researching players in the search market (and also learning from our previous approach with Meilisearch), we decided on Meilisearch because we knew it fit both of those goals.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;&lt;a href=&quot;https://www.meilisearch.com&quot;&gt;Meilisearch&lt;/a&gt; have a passion for open source software and even has their &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/meilisearch/meilisearch&quot;&gt;search engine built in Rust&lt;/a&gt; which has a tiny footprint and great performance. They’ve &lt;a href=&quot;https://blog.meilisearch.com/v1-enterprise-ready-stable/&quot;&gt;recently announced Meilisearch 1.0&lt;/a&gt; and now also have a cloud offering for those who would like a managed instance.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We decided to partner up with Meilisearch to use their engine and their Meilisearch Cloud offering to host our search engine. They graciously sponsored a hosted plan that they manage for us. This lets us focus on building Tauri, knowing that the maintenance and upkeep of the search engine are in trusted hands.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;search-ingestion--indexing&quot;&gt;Search Ingestion &amp;#x26; Indexing&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The next piece of the puzzle is to actually get the content indexed and ingested into the search engine. &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/FabianLars&quot;&gt;Fabian&lt;/a&gt; from the Tauri Working Group set up the project using the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/meilisearch/docs-scraper&quot;&gt;docs-scraper&lt;/a&gt; project from Meilisearch.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Previously, we had a setup that would take our markdown documentation and JavaScript AST and use that to build the search index. Although it led to much faster indexing times, it meant we had a tightly coupled dependency between search indexing and the way we rendered content on our website. This would sometimes lead to the search results getting out of sync with the content on the live website. In order to update the website or optimize the indexer, someone would need to have knowledge of both, making it more difficult to maintain.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;With the current version of the scraper, we can handle all of the configuration and tweaking of results with a &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri-docs/blob/dev/scraper.json&quot;&gt;single JSON file&lt;/a&gt; and let the scraper take care of the rest. We set up a GitHub action that would perform the scraping as part of our CI/CD pipeline and then send those results to our Meilisearch Cloud instance.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;search-frontend&quot;&gt;Search Frontend&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The final step was to create the frontend UI that people visiting &lt;a href=&quot;https://tauri.app&quot;&gt;tauri.app&lt;/a&gt; would interact with. &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/amrbashir&quot;&gt;Amr&lt;/a&gt; from the Tauri Working Group created the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/meilisearch-docsearch&quot;&gt;meilisearch-docsearch&lt;/a&gt; project to help with this. This repo is compatible with Meilisearch 1.0 (we’re using it now on &lt;a href=&quot;https://tauri.app&quot;&gt;tauri.app&lt;/a&gt;).&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;It’s inspired by the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/algolia/docsearch/&quot;&gt;algolia/docsearch&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/meilisearch/docs-searchbar.js/&quot;&gt;meilisearch/docs-searchbar.js&lt;/a&gt; projects that provided a very solid foundation to begin with. The beauty of open source is that we can learn from each other and use that to give back to the ecosystem.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;To talk through some of the details, I’ll hand it over to Amr:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;hr&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/meilisearch/docs-searchbar.js/&quot;&gt;meilisearch/docs-searchbar.js&lt;/a&gt; project was good, but I always felt it could use some improvements to get feature parity with &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/algolia/docsearch/&quot;&gt;algolia/docsearch&lt;/a&gt;. I also felt we could help with an update to help with UI/UX.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The main pain points with &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/meilisearch/docs-searchbar.js/&quot;&gt;meilisearch/docs-searchbar.js&lt;/a&gt; were:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;ol&gt;
&lt;li&gt;On mobile screens, it needed UI/UX improvements so that the search results wouldn’t go off-screen.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;On desktop, the search results would normally appear under the search input, which in the case of &lt;a href=&quot;https://tauri.app&quot;&gt;tauri.app&lt;/a&gt; was placed on the top-right. This caused a bad UX because you’d have to keep moving your eyes between the page content in the middle and the search results in the top-right.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;It is missing a common keybinding. &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;ctrl/command + K&lt;/code&gt; to start the search, &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;ctrl/command + K&lt;/code&gt; is pretty common in the JS ecosystem documentation sites. It also doesn’t have the ability to select text on the page and then trigger a search directly from that text.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ol&gt;
&lt;p&gt;I have been always a big fan of &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/algolia/docsearch/&quot;&gt;algolia/docsearch&lt;/a&gt; UI/UX. It checked all the features on my list, and I always wanted to have that for &lt;a href=&quot;https://tauri.app&quot;&gt;tauri.app&lt;/a&gt;. In fact, several months ago, I tried to change the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/meilisearch/docs-searchbar.js/&quot;&gt;meilisearch/docs-searchbar.js&lt;/a&gt; CSS on our end to improve point 1 and 2 above but I stopped mid-way because of the difficulty of building on top of existing css and fighting for highest specifity (plus CSS is hard :wink:). Other projects also couldn’t benefit from our modifications in an easy way.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Later, we were discussing how to improve the search UI and UX, and we decided that we could improve on the base Meilisearch UI. That project turned into &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/meilisearch-docsearch&quot;&gt;meilisearch-docsearch&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h3 id=&quot;seeing-it-in-action&quot;&gt;Seeing It In Action&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The good news is that all of these changes are live! You can see the new search on &lt;a href=&quot;https://tauri.app&quot;&gt;tauri.app&lt;/a&gt;. We’d love to hear your feedback on what works and what could use improvement in the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri-docs/discussions/1115&quot;&gt;GitHub Discussion for this blog post&lt;/a&gt;. We invite you to not only report bugs, but also if your search result wasn’t expected or should be higher up on the list of results.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;&lt;img alt=&quot;Search Preview&quot; loading=&quot;lazy&quot; decoding=&quot;async&quot; width=&quot;544&quot; height=&quot;494&quot; src=&quot;https://v2.tauri.app/_astro/search_preview.vAj7BCCo_Z1NMQIX.webp&quot; srcset=&quot;&quot;&gt;&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;We’d like to wrap up by extending a special thank you to &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/yankeeinlondon&quot;&gt;Ken&lt;/a&gt; for the original Tauri Search project, &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/amrbashir&quot;&gt;Amr&lt;/a&gt; for the &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/meilisearch-docsearch&quot;&gt;meilisearch-docsearch&lt;/a&gt; project, &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/FabianLars&quot;&gt;Fabian&lt;/a&gt; for writing the revised index scraper and continuous testing, Meilisearch for their partnership and the Meilisearch Cloud instance and finally the Tauri community for their continual feedback to help us drive Tauri and the community forward.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;next-steps&quot;&gt;Next Steps&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;Over the next year, the Tauri Working Group is excited to continue working towards Tauri 2.0. We also have a website rewrite in the works that we’ll be launching in beta this spring. If you’d like to get involved, you can reach out to us in the &lt;a href=&quot;https://discord.com/invite/tauri&quot;&gt;Tauri Discord&lt;/a&gt; and follow us on &lt;a href=&quot;https://fosstodon.org/@TauriApps&quot;&gt;Mastodon&lt;/a&gt; and &lt;a href=&quot;https://twitter.com/TauriApps&quot;&gt;Twitter&lt;/a&gt;.&lt;/p&gt;
</content:encoded></item><item><title>Migration to webkit2gtk-4.1 on Linux port</title><link>https://v2.tauri.app/blog/tauri-2-0-0-alpha-3/</link><guid isPermaLink="true">https://v2.tauri.app/blog/tauri-2-0-0-alpha-3/</guid><description>New v2.0 alpha released and new backend for Linux port</description><pubDate>Fri, 03 Feb 2023 00:00:00 GMT</pubDate><content:encoded>&lt;p&gt;Hello everybody! We just released &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/tauri/releases/tag/tauri-v2.0.0-alpha.3&quot;&gt;Tauri v2.0.0-alpha3&lt;/a&gt; recently. While it doesn’t bring any major feature, it does bring some huge impacts on Linux port.
We will use &lt;a href=&quot;https://webkitgtk.org/reference/webkit2gtk/stable/index.html&quot;&gt;WebKit2GTK–4.1&lt;/a&gt; in 2.0 from now on.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;what-does-this-mean&quot;&gt;What does this mean?&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;If you are using Tauri version 1.x, there’s nothing to worry about. Everything you need is still the same.
But if you are using Tauri version 2.0 alpha version starting from &lt;code dir=&quot;auto&quot;&gt;alpha.3&lt;/code&gt;, you will need to install the new WebKit2GTK package with API version 4.1.
We will update the prerequisites in our site soon. But if you want to know how to install such version, here are some instructions from &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/wry#linux&quot;&gt;wry&lt;/a&gt;:&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;figure&gt;&lt;figcaption&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/figcaption&gt;&lt;pre&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;# On Arch Linux / Manjaro:&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;sudo&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;pacman&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;-S&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;webkit2gtk-4.1&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;# On Debian / Ubuntu:&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;sudo&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;apt&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;install&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;libwebkit2gtk-4.1-dev&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;# On Fedora:&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span&gt;sudo&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;dnf&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;install&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt; &lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;webkit2gtk4.1-devel&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/figure&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;will-this-bring-breaking-changes-to-my-code&quot;&gt;Will this bring breaking changes to my code?&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The main difference between version 4.0 and 4.1 are the soup library. WebKit2GTK-4.0 uses soup2 and WebKit2GTK-4.1 uses soup3.
So if you didn’t use any soup2-specific APIs, your applications should continue to work fine.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The reason behind this change is because we aim to add flatpak support, but &lt;a href=&quot;https://docs.flatpak.org/en/latest/available-runtimes.html#gnome&quot;&gt;Gnome runtime&lt;/a&gt; uses webkit2gtk-4.1.
There are also some subtle bugs like &lt;a href=&quot;https://github.com/tauri-apps/wry/issues/717&quot;&gt;this&lt;/a&gt; that only happen in soup2 and they can be fixed by upgrading to soup3.&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;div&gt;&lt;h2 id=&quot;what-other-breaking-changes-we-are-going-to-expect&quot;&gt;What other breaking changes we are going to expect?&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;p&gt;The major one will be the MSRV. With Tauri v2.0.0-alpha.3 released, MSRV is bumped to 1.64. We will also update windows-rs in the future.
This Rust version should satisfy the latest version of windows-rs.
We do plan to update our MSRV with minor releases after 2.0. This could ease the friction to stick to any fixed Rust version while updating dependencies.&lt;/p&gt;
</content:encoded></item></channel></rss>
